## 9.3 Catalog and role model

### PostgreSQL assumptions

- Role discovery mode is catalog discovery, not a fixed-role-only inventory.
- Deny-by-default privilege posture is enabled.
- Live-unknown representation kinds: unknown_until_live_preflight, unknown_until_runtime_revalidation

### Dynamic role-discovery rules

```json
{
  "discovery_primitive_ref": "PRIMITIVE-ROLE-DISCOVERY",

  "prohibited_roles": [],
  "required_roles": [
    "research_governance",
    "research_test",

}
```

- Evaluate every discovered role.
- Required roles must be present.
- Special semantic roles include PUBLIC and postgres as declared.

### Natural-identity rules

- Natural identity uses declared identity field paths only.
- OID, object_oid, relation_oid, function_oid, row_number, physical_location, and ctid are prohibited as identity fields.

### Unstable-field prohibitions

- Unstable physical identifiers and OID-derived fields are prohibited in natural identity and digest identity contracts.

## 9.4 Privilege architecture

### Privilege paths

- direct
- PUBLIC
- inherited
- SET ROLE-only
- owner-derived
- superuser-derived
- schema-gated
- exercisable

### Schemas

- Schema privileges are evaluated through schema privilege and ownership inventory primitives.
- Dimensions: USAGE, CREATE, CONNECT, EXECUTE, SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE, TRUNCATE, REFERENCES, TRIGGER, SET_ROLE

### Functions

Function ACL:

```text
COALESCE(proacl, acldefault('f', proowner))
```

```json
{
  "acl_resolution": "coalesce_proacl_acldefault_f_proowner",
  "overload_specific_identity": true,
  "public_execute_supported": true,
  "schema_usage_gating": true
}
```

### Tables

Table ACL:

```text
COALESCE(relacl, acldefault('r', relowner))
```

```json
{
  "acl_resolution": "acldefault_r_owner",
  "owner_default_privilege_provenance": true
}
```

### Sequences

Sequence ACL:

```text
COALESCE(relacl, acldefault('s', relowner))
```

```json
{
  "acl_resolution": "acldefault_s_owner",
  "owner_default_privilege_provenance": true
}
```

### Columns

Column ACL:

```text
NULL attacl means no column-specific ACL.
acldefault('c', ...) is prohibited.
```

```json
{
  "acldefault_column_prohibited": true,
  "effective_column_rules": {
    "decision_refs": [
      "ND-DIGEST-CHECKPOINT-V1"
    ],
    "mode": "empty_column_acl_set_with_effective_from_table"
  },
  "null_attacl_means": "no_column_specific_acl",
  "table_privileges_independently_authoritative": true
}
```

- Default privilege records present: 6
- Owner-derived default privilege provenance is part of the privilege model.

## 9.7 Mapping and state proofs

### Exactly-one outcome-slot contract

- Each reconciled source must map to exactly one outcome slot under the ordered cascade.
- Zero-slot and multi-slot violations are fail-closed and covered by mapping fixtures.

### Shared-event source-reference rules

- Shared-event natural keys and source-domain primitives constrain event linkage.
- Mapping fixtures enumerate allowed and fail-closed shared-event cases.

### State A identity sets

- State A fixture count: 14
- `FIXTURE-STATEA-ALG-MISMATCH` → expected state `{"data_type":"string","kind":"deterministic_value","value":"algorithm_version_mismatch"}`
- `FIXTURE-STATEA-COUNT-NE-ID` → expected state `{"data_type":"string","kind":"deterministic_value","value":"equal_counts_unequal_identity_sets"}`
- `FIXTURE-STATEA-DIGEST-MISMATCH` → expected state `{"data_type":"string","kind":"deterministic_value","value":"set_digest_mismatch"}`
- `FIXTURE-STATEA-DISC-ONLY` → expected state `{"data_type":"string","kind":"deterministic_value","value":"source_present_only_in_discrepancy"}`
- `FIXTURE-STATEA-DUP-NATURAL` → expected state `{"data_type":"string","kind":"deterministic_value","value":"duplicate_natural_identity"}`
- `FIXTURE-STATEA-FALLBACK-ONLY` → expected state `{"data_type":"string","kind":"deterministic_value","value":"source_present_only_in_fallback"}`
- `FIXTURE-STATEA-MISSING-CLASSIFIED` → expected state `{"data_type":"string","kind":"deterministic_value","value":"missing_classified_source"}`
- `FIXTURE-STATEA-MULTI-CLASSIFIED` → expected state `{"data_type":"string","kind":"deterministic_value","value":"multiply_classified_source"}`
- `FIXTURE-STATEA-ORDER-MUT` → expected state `{"data_type":"string","kind":"deterministic_value","value":"canonical_order_mutation"}`
- `FIXTURE-STATEA-RULE-ORDER` → expected state `{"data_type":"string","kind":"deterministic_value","value":"rule_order_change_after_classification"}`
- `FIXTURE-STATEA-STALE` → expected state `{"data_type":"string","kind":"deterministic_value","value":"stale_cached_classification"}`
- `FIXTURE-STATEA-UNKNOWN-ARCHIVE` → expected state `{"data_type":"string","kind":"deterministic_value","value":"unknown_archive_reference"}`
- `FIXTURE-STATEA-UNKNOWN-EVENT` → expected state `{"data_type":"string","kind":"deterministic_value","value":"unknown_event_reference"}`
- `FIXTURE-STATEA-VALID` → expected state `{"data_type":"string","kind":"deterministic_value","value":"valid_identity_set_equality"}`

### State B independent recomputation requirements

- State B fixture count: 13
- Invalid State B cases must resolve to failed_frozen.
- `FIXTURE-STATEB-ALG-MISMATCH` → expected state `{"data_type":"string","kind":"deterministic_value","value":"failed_frozen"}`
- `FIXTURE-STATEB-ARCHIVE-CHANGED` → expected state `{"data_type":"string","kind":"deterministic_value","value":"failed_frozen"}`
- `FIXTURE-STATEB-COUNT-SWAP` → expected state `{"data_type":"string","kind":"deterministic_value","value":"failed_frozen"}`
- `FIXTURE-STATEB-CP-PRESENT-FAIL` → expected state `{"data_type":"string","kind":"deterministic_value","value":"failed_frozen"}`
- `FIXTURE-STATEB-DISC-CHANGED` → expected state `{"data_type":"string","kind":"deterministic_value","value":"failed_frozen"}`
- `FIXTURE-STATEB-EVENT-REMOVED` → expected state `{"data_type":"string","kind":"deterministic_value","value":"failed_frozen"}`
- `FIXTURE-STATEB-FALLBACK-CHANGED` → expected state `{"data_type":"string","kind":"deterministic_value","value":"failed_frozen"}`
- `FIXTURE-STATEB-FORGED-DIGEST` → expected state `{"data_type":"string","kind":"deterministic_value","value":"failed_frozen"}`
- `FIXTURE-STATEB-LEGACY-ADDED` → expected state `{"data_type":"string","kind":"deterministic_value","value":"failed_frozen"}`
- `FIXTURE-STATEB-NORM-CHANGED` → expected state `{"data_type":"string","kind":"deterministic_value","value":"failed_frozen"}`
- `FIXTURE-STATEB-SHARED-EVENT` → expected state `{"data_type":"string","kind":"deterministic_value","value":"failed_frozen"}`
- `FIXTURE-STATEB-UNKNOWN-SOURCE` → expected state `{"data_type":"string","kind":"deterministic_value","value":"failed_frozen"}`
- `FIXTURE-STATEB-VALID` → expected state `{"data_type":"string","kind":"deterministic_value","value":"valid_independent_recomputation"}`

### Failure behavior

- Fail closed on mapping, State A, and State B violations.
- Fallback RULE-22 yields failed_frozen when no earlier rule matches and required status is missing.

## 9.8 Execution gates

### Live-preflight gates (11)

- `MARKER-COLUMN-ACLS`
- `MARKER-COLUMN-EFFECTIVE`
- `MARKER-DEFAULT-PRIVS`
- `MARKER-FUNCTION-ACLS`
- `MARKER-FUNCTION-EFFECTIVE`
- `MARKER-FUNCTION-PUBLIC`
- `MARKER-MEMBERSHIPS`
- `MARKER-ROLES`
- `MARKER-SCHEMA-PRIVS`
- `MARKER-TABLE-ACLS`
- `MARKER-TABLE-WRITES`

### Runtime-revalidation gates (3)

- `MARKER-DRIFT-END`
- `MARKER-DRIFT-START`
- `MARKER-SAFETY-BASELINE`

### Allowed comparison outcomes

- MATCHES_RECONSTRUCTED_REFERENCE
- MISSING_REQUIRED_OBJECT
- OWNER_MISMATCH
- PRIVILEGE_EXPANSION
- PRIVILEGE_REDUCTION
- RUNTIME_STATE_MISMATCH
- SAFE_DECLARED_VARIANCE
- UNSAFE_UNDECLARED_VARIANCE

### Fail-closed behavior

- Missing required objects, unsafe undeclared variance, privilege expansion, and runtime state mismatch fail closed.

### Prohibition on automatic privilege repair

- Automatic privilege repair is prohibited; owner action is required for privilege discrepancies.

## 9.9 Security invariants

- No live provider/model calls during offline validation or documentation generation.
- No paid usage.
- No workflow activation.
- No secret extraction.
- No schema-9 execution before preflight.
- No pilot before runtime revalidation.
- No silent extra-object acceptance.
- No SENTINEL modification.
- No Equitify access.
